+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: Copyright contributors to the kvcached project
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+"""End-to-end benchmark for kvcached + TriAttention.
+
+This runner compares vLLM in three optional modes:
+
+  native       - no kvcached, no TriAttention
+  kvcached     - kvcached elastic KV only
+  triattention - kvcached elastic KV plus TriAttention compression
+
+It intentionally starts a fresh vLLM server for every cell so peak memory,
+logs, and compression events are easy to attribute. Requests use the OpenAI
+compatible streaming completions endpoint, allowing client-side TTFT/TPOT
+measurement without depending on vLLM's benchmark CLI.
+
+Example:
+    python tools/triattention_benchmark.py \
+        --model /models/Qwen3-8B \
+        --labels kvcached,triattention \
+        --workloads single,concurrency \
+        --max-tokens-list 512,1024,2048,4096 \
+        --concurrency-list 1,2,4 \
+        --kv-budget-list 1024,2048
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import csv
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import shutil
+import signal
+import statistics
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import threading
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_STATS_PATH = REPO_ROOT / "kvcached" / "triattention" / "vllm" / "stats" / "qwen3_32b_int4_stats.pt"
+
+CSV_COLUMNS = [
+    "label",
+    "workload",
+    "model",
+    "prompt_repeat",
+    "kv_budget",
+    "divide_length",
+    "max_tokens",
+    "concurrency",
+    "requests",
+    "successes",
+    "failures",
+    "success_rate",
+    "elapsed_secs",
+    "ttft_mean_ms",
+    "ttft_p50_ms",
+    "ttft_p99_ms",
+    "tpot_mean_ms",
+    "e2e_mean_ms",
+    "e2e_p50_ms",
+    "output_chunks",
+    "output_chunks_per_sec",
+    "gpu_mib_before",
+    "gpu_mib_peak",
+    "gpu_mib_avg",
+    "gpu_mib_p95",
+    "gpu_mib_after",
+    "gpu_mib_delta_peak",
+    "compression_events",
+    "free_blocks_events",
+    "freed_blocks",
+    "triattention_activation_seen",
+    "error_count",
+    "log_path",
+]
+
+
+@dataclass
+class RequestResult:
+    ok: bool
+    ttft_s: float | None
+    e2e_s: float
+    output_chunks: int
+    output_chars: int
+    text_head: str
+    error: str | None = None
+    expected_ok: bool | None = None
+
+
+@dataclass
+class LogMetrics:
+    compression_events: int
+    free_blocks_events: int
+    freed_blocks: int
+    triattention_activation_seen: bool
+    error_count: int
+
+
+class GPUMemorySampler:
+    def __init__(self, interval_s: float, gpu_index: str | None) -> None:
+        self.interval_s = interval_s
+        self.gpu_index = gpu_index
+        self.samples: list[int] = []
+        self._stop = threading.Event()
+        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
+
+    def start(self) -> None:
+        self._thread.start()
+
+    def stop(self) -> None:
+        self._stop.set()
+        self._thread.join(timeout=max(2.0, self.interval_s * 4))
+
+    @property
+    def peak(self) -> int | None:
+        return max(self.samples) if self.samples else None
+
+    @property
+    def avg(self) -> float | None:
+        return statistics.mean(self.samples) if self.samples else None
+
+    @property
+    def p95(self) -> float | None:
+        return percentile([float(sample) for sample in self.samples], 95)
+
+    def _run(self) -> None:
+        while not self._stop.is_set():
+            value = query_gpu_memory_mib(self.gpu_index)
+            if value is not None:
+                self.samples.append(value)
+            self._stop.wait(self.interval_s)
+
+
+def parse_int_list(raw: str) -> list[int]:
+    items = [part.strip() for part in raw.replace(" ", ",").split(",") if part.strip()]
+    return [int(item) for item in items]
+
+
+def parse_str_list(raw: str) -> list[str]:
+    return [part.strip() for part in raw.replace(" ", ",").split(",") if part.strip()]
+
+
+def percentile(values: list[float], pct: float) -> float | None:
+    if not values:
+        return None
+    if len(values) == 1:
+        return values[0]
+    ordered = sorted(values)
+    rank = (len(ordered) - 1) * pct / 100.0
+    lo = int(rank)
+    hi = min(lo + 1, len(ordered) - 1)
+    frac = rank - lo
+    return ordered[lo] * (1.0 - frac) + ordered[hi] * frac
+
+
+def fmt_float(value: float | None, digits: int = 3) -> str:
+    if value is None:
+        return ""
+    return f"{value:.{digits}f}"
+
+
+def query_gpu_memory_mib(gpu_index: str | None) -> int | None:
+    if shutil.which("nvidia-smi") is None:
+        return None
+    cmd = [
+        "nvidia-smi",
+        "--query-gpu=memory.used",
+        "--format=csv,noheader,nounits",
+    ]
+    if gpu_index:
+        cmd.extend(["-i", gpu_index])
+    try:
+        out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=5)
+    except Exception:
+        return None
+    for line in out.splitlines():
+        line = line.strip()
+        if not line:
+            continue
+        try:
+            return int(line)
+        except ValueError:
+            return None
+    return None
+
+
+def wait_ready(port: int, pid: int, timeout_s: int) -> bool:
+    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_s
+    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/v1/models"
+    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
+        try:
+            os.kill(pid, 0)
+        except OSError:
+            return False
+        try:
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=2) as resp:
+                if 200 <= resp.status < 500:
+                    return True
+        except Exception:
+            time.sleep(2)
+    return False
+
+
+def stop_process(proc: subprocess.Popen[Any]) -> None:
+    if proc.poll() is not None:
+        return
+    try:
+        proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+    except Exception:
+        try:
+            proc.kill()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        try:
+            proc.wait(timeout=5)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+
+
+def label_env(label: str, kv_budget: int, divide_length: int, stats_path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
+    env: dict[str, str] = {}
+    if label in {"kvcached", "triattention"}:
+        env.update(
+            {
+                "ENABLE_KVCACHED": "1",
+                "KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH": "1",
+            }
+        )
+    if label in {"native", "kvcached"}:
+        env.update(
+            {
+                "ENABLE_TRIATTENTION": "0",
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_DISABLE_COMPRESSION": "1",
+            }
+        )
+    if label == "triattention":
+        env.update(
+            {
+                "ENABLE_TRIATTENTION": "1",
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET": str(kv_budget),
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_DIVIDE_LENGTH": str(divide_length),
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH": str(stats_path),
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_LOG_DECISIONS": "1",
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_KV_COMPACTION": "true",
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BLOCK_RECLAIM": "true",
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_REQUIRE_PHYSICAL_RECLAIM": "true",
+            }
+        )
+    return env
+
+
+def build_vllm_command(args: argparse.Namespace) -> list[str]:
+    cmd = [
+        args.vllm_bin,
+        "serve",
+        args.model,
+        "--served-model-name",
+        args.served_model_name,
+        "--dtype",
+        args.dtype,
+        "--max-model-len",
+        str(args.max_model_len),
+        "--trust-remote-code",
+        "--no-enable-prefix-caching",
+        "--gpu-memory-utilization",
+        str(args.gpu_memory_utilization),
+        "--port",
+        str(args.port),
+    ]
+    if args.enforce_eager:
+        cmd.append("--enforce-eager")
+    cmd.extend(args.extra_vllm_arg or [])
+    return cmd
+
+
+def build_generation_prompt(prompt_repeat: int, request_id: int) -> str:
+    seed = (
+        "This is deterministic benchmark context for long generation. "
+        "The model should continue a numbered synthetic list without stopping early. "
+    )
+    context = seed * prompt_repeat
+    return (
+        f"{context}\n"
+        f"Request id: {request_id}.\n"
+        "Write an extended numbered list. Each line must start with ITEM and the next "
+        "integer. Keep going until the token limit is reached. Do not summarize."
+    )
+
+
+def build_needle_prompt(prompt_repeat: int, request_id: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
+    secret = f"TRIATTN-CODE-{request_id:04d}"
+    filler = (
+        "Filler context about memory systems, schedulers, and attention kernels. "
+        "This sentence carries no answer. "
+    )
+    before = filler * max(1, prompt_repeat // 2)
+    after = filler * max(1, prompt_repeat - prompt_repeat // 2)
+    prompt = (
+        f"{before}\n"
+        f"The secret code is {secret}.\n"
+        f"{after}\n"
+        "What is the secret code? Answer with only the code."
+    )
+    return prompt, secret
+
+
+def stream_completion(
+    *,
+    port: int,
+    model: str,
+    prompt: str,
+    max_tokens: int,
+    temperature: float,
+    ignore_eos: bool,
+    request_timeout_s: int,
+    expected_substring: str | None = None,
+) -> RequestResult:
+    body = {
+        "model": model,
+        "prompt": prompt,
+        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
+        "temperature": temperature,
+        "stream": True,
+    }
+    if ignore_eos:
+        body["ignore_eos"] = True
+    data = json.dumps(body).encode("utf-8")
+    req = urllib.request.Request(
+        f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/v1/completions",
+        data=data,
+        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
+        method="POST",
+    )
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    first_token_at: float | None = None
+    chunks = 0
+    text_parts: list[str] = []
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=request_timeout_s) as resp:
+            for raw_line in resp:
+                line = raw_line.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
+                if not line or not line.startswith("data:"):
+                    continue
+                payload = line[5:].strip()
+                if payload == "[DONE]":
+                    break
+                try:
+                    event = json.loads(payload)
+                except json.JSONDecodeError:
+                    continue
+                choices = event.get("choices") or []
+                if not choices:
+                    continue
+                text = choices[0].get("text") or ""
+                if text:
+                    if first_token_at is None:
+                        first_token_at = time.perf_counter()
+                    chunks += 1
+                    text_parts.append(text)
+        end = time.perf_counter()
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
+        err = exc.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")[:1000]
+        return RequestResult(
+            ok=False,
+            ttft_s=None,
+            e2e_s=time.perf_counter() - start,
+            output_chunks=0,
+            output_chars=0,
+            text_head="",
+            error=f"HTTP {exc.code}: {err}",
+        )
+    except Exception as exc:
+        return RequestResult(
+            ok=False,
+            ttft_s=None,
+            e2e_s=time.perf_counter() - start,
+            output_chunks=0,
+            output_chars=0,
+            text_head="",
+            error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
+        )
+    text_all = "".join(text_parts)
+    expected_ok = None
+    if expected_substring is not None:
+        expected_ok = expected_substring in text_all
+    return RequestResult(
+        ok=chunks > 0 and expected_ok is not False,
+        ttft_s=(first_token_at - start) if first_token_at is not None else None,
+        e2e_s=end - start,
+        output_chunks=chunks,
+        output_chars=len(text_all),
+        text_head=text_all[:200].replace("\n", "\\n"),
+        expected_ok=expected_ok,
+    )
+
+
+def parse_log_metrics(log_path: Path) -> LogMetrics:
+    if not log_path.exists():
+        return LogMetrics(0, 0, 0, False, 0)
+    text = log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
+    compression_patterns = [
+        r"TriAttention compression events",
+        r"TriAttention compression applied",
+        r"TriAttention compressing req=",
+        r"TriAttention update_from_output: received",
+    ]
+    compression_events = sum(len(re.findall(pattern, text)) for pattern in compression_patterns)
+    free_blocks_events = len(re.findall(r"TriAttention scheduler FREE_BLOCKS", text))
+    freed_blocks = sum(int(match) for match in re.findall(r"freed=(\d+)", text))
+    activation_seen = (
+        "Runtime plugin activated" in text
+        or "Installed TriAttention runtime monkeypatch integration" in text
+        or "TriAttention scheduler hooks initialized" in text
+    )
+    error_count = len(
+        re.findall(
+            r"Traceback|ERROR|CRITICAL|OutOfMemory|TRIATTN_FATAL|compaction_failed",
+            text,
+        )
+    )
+    return LogMetrics(
+        compression_events=compression_events,
+        free_blocks_events=free_blocks_events,
+        freed_blocks=freed_blocks,
+        triattention_activation_seen=activation_seen,
+        error_count=error_count,
+    )
+
+
+def summarize_results(
+    *,
+    args: argparse.Namespace,
+    label: str,
+    workload: str,
+    kv_budget: int,
+    results: list[RequestResult],
+    elapsed_s: float,
+    gpu_before: int | None,
+    gpu_peak: int | None,
+    gpu_avg: float | None,
+    gpu_p95: float | None,
+    gpu_after: int | None,
+    log_metrics: LogMetrics,
+    log_path: Path,
+    max_tokens: int,
+    concurrency: int,
+) -> dict[str, str]:
+    successes = sum(1 for result in results if result.ok)
+    failures = len(results) - successes
+    ttfts_ms = [result.ttft_s * 1000.0 for result in results if result.ok and result.ttft_s is not None]
+    e2es_ms = [result.e2e_s * 1000.0 for result in results if result.ok]
+    output_chunks = sum(result.output_chunks for result in results if result.ok)
+    per_request_tpots_ms = []
+    for result in results:
+        if not result.ok or result.ttft_s is None or result.output_chunks <= 1:
+            continue
+        decode_s = max(0.0, result.e2e_s - result.ttft_s)
+        per_request_tpots_ms.append((decode_s / (result.output_chunks - 1)) * 1000.0)
+
+    return {
+        "label": label,
+        "workload": workload,
+        "model": args.model,
+        "prompt_repeat": str(args.prompt_repeat),
+        "kv_budget": str(kv_budget) if label == "triattention" else "",
+        "divide_length": str(args.divide_length) if label == "triattention" else "",
+        "max_tokens": str(max_tokens),
+        "concurrency": str(concurrency),
+        "requests": str(len(results)),
+        "successes": str(successes),
+        "failures": str(failures),
+        "success_rate": fmt_float(successes / len(results) if results else 0.0, 4),
+        "elapsed_secs": fmt_float(elapsed_s),
+        "ttft_mean_ms": fmt_float(statistics.mean(ttfts_ms) if ttfts_ms else None),
+        "ttft_p50_ms": fmt_float(percentile(ttfts_ms, 50)),
+        "ttft_p99_ms": fmt_float(percentile(ttfts_ms, 99)),
+        "tpot_mean_ms": fmt_float(statistics.mean(per_request_tpots_ms) if per_request_tpots_ms else None),
+        "e2e_mean_ms": fmt_float(statistics.mean(e2es_ms) if e2es_ms else None),
+        "e2e_p50_ms": fmt_float(percentile(e2es_ms, 50)),
+        "output_chunks": str(output_chunks),
+        "output_chunks_per_sec": fmt_float(output_chunks / elapsed_s if elapsed_s > 0 else 0.0),
+        "gpu_mib_before": str(gpu_before) if gpu_before is not None else "",
+        "gpu_mib_peak": str(gpu_peak) if gpu_peak is not None else "",
+        "gpu_mib_avg": fmt_float(gpu_avg),
+        "gpu_mib_p95": fmt_float(gpu_p95),
+        "gpu_mib_after": str(gpu_after) if gpu_after is not None else "",
+        "gpu_mib_delta_peak": (
+            str(gpu_peak - gpu_before)
+            if gpu_peak is not None and gpu_before is not None
+            else ""
+        ),
+        "compression_events": str(log_metrics.compression_events),
+        "free_blocks_events": str(log_metrics.free_blocks_events),
+        "freed_blocks": str(log_metrics.freed_blocks),
+        "triattention_activation_seen": str(log_metrics.triattention_activation_seen).lower(),
+        "error_count": str(log_metrics.error_count),
+        "log_path": str(log_path),
+    }
+
+
+def run_requests_for_cell(
+    *,
+    args: argparse.Namespace,
+    workload: str,
+    max_tokens: int,
+    concurrency: int,
+) -> list[RequestResult]:
+    ignore_eos = args.ignore_eos and workload != "needle"
+    request_count = concurrency if workload != "single" else 1
+
+    def one_request(index: int) -> RequestResult:
+        expected = None
+        if workload == "needle":
+            prompt, expected = build_needle_prompt(args.prompt_repeat, index)
+            request_max_tokens = min(max_tokens, args.needle_max_tokens)
+        else:
+            prompt = build_generation_prompt(args.prompt_repeat, index)
+            request_max_tokens = max_tokens
+        return stream_completion(
+            port=args.port,
+            model=args.served_model_name,
+            prompt=prompt,
+            max_tokens=request_max_tokens,
+            temperature=args.temperature,
+            ignore_eos=ignore_eos,
+            request_timeout_s=args.request_timeout,
+            expected_substring=expected,
+        )
+
+    if request_count == 1:
+        return [one_request(0)]
+    results: list[RequestResult] = []
+    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=concurrency) as executor:
+        futures = [executor.submit(one_request, index) for index in range(request_count)]
+        for future in as_completed(futures):
+            results.append(future.result())
+    return results
+
+
+def run_cell(
+    *,
+    args: argparse.Namespace,
+    label: str,
+    workload: str,
+    kv_budget: int,
+    max_tokens: int,
+    concurrency: int,
+    log_dir: Path,
+) -> dict[str, str]:
+    cell_name = f"{workload}-{label}-budget{kv_budget}-tok{max_tokens}-conc{concurrency}"
+    log_path = log_dir / f"{cell_name}.log"
+    env = os.environ.copy()
+    env.update(label_env(label, kv_budget, args.divide_length, args.stats_path))
+    cmd = build_vllm_command(args)
+    print(f"\n===== {cell_name} =====", flush=True)
+    print("server:", " ".join(cmd), flush=True)
+    print("env:", " ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in label_env(label, kv_budget, args.divide_length, args.stats_path).items()), flush=True)
+
+    with log_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log_f:
+        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=log_f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
+    try:
+        if not wait_ready(args.port, proc.pid, args.server_start_timeout):
+            stop_process(proc)
+            log_metrics = parse_log_metrics(log_path)
+            print(f"server failed to become ready; log: {log_path}", flush=True)
+            return summarize_results(
+                args=args,
+                label=label,
+                workload=workload,
+                kv_budget=kv_budget,
+                results=[],
+                elapsed_s=0.0,
+                gpu_before=None,
+                gpu_peak=None,
+                gpu_avg=None,
+                gpu_p95=None,
+                gpu_after=None,
+                log_metrics=log_metrics,
+                log_path=log_path,
+                max_tokens=max_tokens,
+                concurrency=concurrency,
+            )
+
+        gpu_before = query_gpu_memory_mib(args.gpu_index)
+        sampler = GPUMemorySampler(args.gpu_sample_interval, args.gpu_index)
+        sampler.start()
+        start = time.perf_counter()
+        results = run_requests_for_cell(
+            args=args,
+            workload=workload,
+            max_tokens=max_tokens,
+            concurrency=concurrency,
+        )
+        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
+        sampler.stop()
+        gpu_after = query_gpu_memory_mib(args.gpu_index)
+        gpu_peak = sampler.peak
+        gpu_avg = sampler.avg
+        gpu_p95 = sampler.p95
+        log_metrics = parse_log_metrics(log_path)
+        row = summarize_results(
+            args=args,
+            label=label,
+            workload=workload,
+            kv_budget=kv_budget,
+            results=results,
+            elapsed_s=elapsed,
+            gpu_before=gpu_before,
+            gpu_peak=gpu_peak,
+            gpu_avg=gpu_avg,
+            gpu_p95=gpu_p95,
+            gpu_after=gpu_after,
+            log_metrics=log_metrics,
+            log_path=log_path,
+            max_tokens=max_tokens,
+            concurrency=concurrency,
+        )
+        print(
+            "summary: "
+            f"success={row['successes']}/{row['requests']} "
+            f"chunks/s={row['output_chunks_per_sec']} "
+            f"peak_mib={row['gpu_mib_peak']} "
+            f"compression_events={row['compression_events']} "
+            f"free_blocks={row['free_blocks_events']}",
+            flush=True,
+        )
+        failed = [result for result in results if not result.ok and result.error]
+        if failed:
+            print(f"first request error: {failed[0].error}", flush=True)
+        return row
+    finally:
+        stop_process(proc)
+        time.sleep(args.cooldown_secs)
+
+
+def build_cells(args: argparse.Namespace) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
+    workloads = parse_str_list(args.workloads)
+    cells: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
+    if "single" in workloads:
+        for max_tokens in parse_int_list(args.max_tokens_list):
+            cells.append(("single", max_tokens, 1))
+    if "concurrency" in workloads:
+        for concurrency in parse_int_list(args.concurrency_list):
+            cells.append(("concurrency", args.concurrency_max_tokens, concurrency))
+    if "needle" in workloads:
+        cells.append(("needle", args.needle_max_tokens, 1))
+    unknown = set(workloads) - {"single", "concurrency", "needle"}
+    if unknown:
+        raise ValueError(f"unknown workloads: {', '.join(sorted(unknown))}")
+    return cells
+
+
+def parse_args(argv: list[str]) -> argparse.Namespace:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
+    parser.add_argument("--model", default=os.environ.get("MODEL", "Qwen/Qwen3-8B"))
+    parser.add_argument("--served-model-name", default=os.environ.get("SERVED_MODEL_NAME", os.environ.get("MODEL", "Qwen/Qwen3-8B")))
+    parser.add_argument("--labels", default=os.environ.get("LABELS", "kvcached,triattention"))
+    parser.add_argument("--workloads", default=os.environ.get("WORKLOADS", "single,concurrency"))
+    parser.add_argument("--max-tokens-list", default=os.environ.get("MAX_TOKENS_LIST", "512,1024,2048,4096"))
+    parser.add_argument("--concurrency-list", default=os.environ.get("CONCURRENCY_LIST", "1,2,4"))
+    parser.add_argument("--concurrency-max-tokens", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("CONCURRENCY_MAX_TOKENS", "4096")))
+    parser.add_argument("--needle-max-tokens", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("NEEDLE_MAX_TOKENS", "64")))
+    parser.add_argument("--prompt-repeat", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("PROMPT_REPEAT", "300")))
+    parser.add_argument("--kv-budget-list", default=os.environ.get("KV_BUDGET_LIST", os.environ.get("KV_BUDGET", "1024")))
+    parser.add_argument("--divide-length", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("DIVIDE_LENGTH", "128")))
+    parser.add_argument("--stats-path", type=Path, default=Path(os.environ.get("STATS_PATH", str(DEFAULT_STATS_PATH))))
+    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8765")))
+    parser.add_argument("--gpu-index", default=os.environ.get("GPU_INDEX"))
+    parser.add_argument("--gpu-memory-utilization", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("GPU_MEM", "0.7")))
+    parser.add_argument("--max-model-len", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("MAX_LEN", "32768")))
+    parser.add_argument("--dtype", default=os.environ.get("DTYPE", "bfloat16"))
+    parser.add_argument("--vllm-bin", default=os.environ.get("VLLM_BIN", "vllm"))
+    parser.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("TEMPERATURE", "0")))
+    parser.add_argument("--request-timeout", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("REQUEST_TIMEOUT", "3600")))
+    parser.add_argument("--server-start-timeout", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("SERVER_START_TIMEOUT", "480")))
+    parser.add_argument("--gpu-sample-interval", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("GPU_SAMPLE_INTERVAL", "0.5")))
+    parser.add_argument("--cooldown-secs", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("COOLDOWN_SECS", "3")))
+    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=Path(os.environ["OUTPUT_DIR"]) if os.environ.get("OUTPUT_DIR") else None)
+    parser.add_argument("--output-csv", type=Path, default=Path(os.environ["OUTPUT_CSV"]) if os.environ.get("OUTPUT_CSV") else None)
+    parser.add_argument("--extra-vllm-arg", action="append", default=[])
+    parser.add_argument("--no-enforce-eager", dest="enforce_eager", action="store_false")
+    parser.set_defaults(enforce_eager=True)
+    parser.add_argument("--no-ignore-eos", dest="ignore_eos", action="store_false")
+    parser.set_defaults(ignore_eos=True)
+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
+    labels = set(parse_str_list(args.labels))
+    unknown_labels = labels - {"native", "kvcached", "triattention"}
+    if unknown_labels:
+        raise SystemExit(f"unknown labels: {', '.join(sorted(unknown_labels))}")
+    return args
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
+    args = parse_args(argv)
+    log_dir = args.output_dir or Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="triattn-bench-"))
+    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    output_csv = args.output_csv or (log_dir / "results.csv")
+    args.stats_path = args.stats_path.resolve()
+
+    labels = parse_str_list(args.labels)
+    kv_budgets = parse_int_list(args.kv_budget_list)
+    cells = build_cells(args)
+
+    print(f"[triattn-bench] output dir: {log_dir}")
+    print(f"[triattn-bench] results CSV: {output_csv}")
+    print(f"[triattn-bench] labels: {labels}")
+    print(f"[triattn-bench] cells: {len(cells)} workloads x {len(labels)} labels x {len(kv_budgets)} budget(s)")
+
+    with output_csv.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=CSV_COLUMNS)
+        writer.writeheader()
+        for workload, max_tokens, concurrency in cells:
+            for kv_budget in kv_budgets:
+                for label in labels:
+                    row = run_cell(
+                        args=args,
+                        label=label,
+                        workload=workload,
+                        kv_budget=kv_budget,
+                        max_tokens=max_tokens,
+                        concurrency=concurrency,
+                        log_dir=log_dir,
+                    )
+                    writer.writerow(row)
+                    f.flush()
+
+    print("\n================ BENCHMARK DONE ================")
+    print(f"results: {output_csv}")
+    print(f"logs   : {log_dir}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

# ===== END 0094.patch =====

diff --git a/tools/triattention_compare.sh b/tools/triattention_compare.sh
new file mode 100644

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: Copyright contributors to the kvcached project
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+#
+# Side-by-side comparison: kvcached alone vs kvcached + TriAttention.
+#
+# Boots vLLM twice on the same MODEL/PORT, runs the same long-context prompt
+# against each, and prints:
+#   - tokens/sec
+#   - peak GPU memory (nvidia-smi)
+#   - number of TriAttention compression events observed in the log
+#
+# Usage:
+#   bash tools/triattention_compare.sh
+#
+# Tweak via env vars (all optional):
+#   MODEL         (default Qwen/Qwen3-8B)
+#   SERVED_MODEL_NAME request/served model name (default: same as MODEL)
+#   PORT          (default 8765)
+#   GPU_MEM       (default 0.7)
+#   MAX_LEN       (default 32768)
+#   KV_BUDGET     TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET (default 512 — small so compression
+#                 actually triggers on a moderate prompt)
+#   PROMPT_REPEAT how many times to repeat the seed sentence (default 200,
+#                 yielding ~1.4k input tokens for Qwen3)
+#   MAX_TOKENS    completion length (default 512)
+
+set -euo pipefail
+
+MODEL="${MODEL:-Qwen/Qwen3-8B}"
+SERVED_MODEL_NAME="${SERVED_MODEL_NAME:-${MODEL}}"
+PORT="${PORT:-8765}"
+GPU_MEM="${GPU_MEM:-0.7}"
+MAX_LEN="${MAX_LEN:-32768}"
+KV_BUDGET="${KV_BUDGET:-512}"
+PROMPT_REPEAT="${PROMPT_REPEAT:-200}"
+MAX_TOKENS="${MAX_TOKENS:-512}"
+
+SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
+REPO_ROOT="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
+STATS_PATH="${STATS_PATH:-${REPO_ROOT}/kvcached/triattention/vllm/stats/qwen3_32b_int4_stats.pt}"
+
+if [[ ! -f "${STATS_PATH}" ]]; then
+    echo "[compare] ERROR: stats file not found: ${STATS_PATH}" >&2
+    echo "[compare] Set STATS_PATH=/path/to/stats.pt and rerun." >&2
+    exit 1
+fi
+
+LOG_DIR="$(mktemp -d -t triattn-cmp-XXXXXX)"
+echo "[compare] logs at ${LOG_DIR}"
+echo "[compare] model: ${MODEL}"
+echo "[compare] served model name: ${SERVED_MODEL_NAME}"
+echo "[compare] stats path: ${STATS_PATH}"
+
+fail() {
+    echo "[compare] ERROR: $*" >&2
+    echo "[compare] logs preserved at ${LOG_DIR}" >&2
+    exit 1
+}
+
+if [[ -n "${PYTHON_BIN:-}" ]]; then
+    PYTHON_CMD="${PYTHON_BIN}"
+elif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+    PYTHON_CMD="python3"
+elif command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+    PYTHON_CMD="python"
+else
+    fail "neither python3 nor python was found on PATH"
+fi
+
+# Build the prompt and JSON body in Python. Bash's ``yes | head`` pattern
+# trips SIGPIPE under ``set -o pipefail`` and can kill the script before any
+# server startup output is printed.
+BODY_FILE="${LOG_DIR}/request.json"
+SERVED_MODEL_NAME="${SERVED_MODEL_NAME}" MAX_TOKENS="${MAX_TOKENS}" PROMPT_REPEAT="${PROMPT_REPEAT}" \
+    "${PYTHON_CMD}" - "${BODY_FILE}" <<'PY' || fail "failed to build request JSON"
+import json, os
+import sys
+seed = "The capital of France is Paris. "
+repeat = int(os.environ["PROMPT_REPEAT"])
+prompt = seed * repeat + "Now in one short sentence, what is the capital of France?"
+body = {
+    "model": os.environ["SERVED_MODEL_NAME"],
+    "prompt": prompt,
+    "max_tokens": int(os.environ["MAX_TOKENS"]),
+    "temperature": 0,
+}
+with open(sys.argv[1], "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+    json.dump(body, f)
+PY
+
+wait_ready() {
+    local timeout=$1 pid=$2 elapsed=0
+    while (( elapsed < timeout )); do
+        if ! kill -0 "${pid}" 2>/dev/null; then return 2; fi
+        if curl -fsS "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/v1/models" >/dev/null 2>&1; then return 0; fi
+        sleep 2; elapsed=$((elapsed + 2))
+    done
+    return 1
+}
+
+gpu_used_mib() {
+    # First GPU; users with multi-GPU should adapt.
+    nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits | head -1 | tr -d ' '
+}
+
+print_start_failure() {
+    local log="$1"
+
+    echo "[compare] likely root-cause snippets from ${log}:"
+    grep -n -E \
+        'Traceback|ERROR|CRITICAL|RuntimeError|ValueError|AssertionError|ImportError|ModuleNotFoundError|CUDA|OutOfMemory|No such file|kvcached|TriAttention' \
+        "${log}" | tail -80 || true
+
+    echo
+    echo "[compare] last 160 log lines from ${log}:"
+    tail -160 "${log}" || true
+}
+
+print_runtime_failure() {
+    local label="$1"
+    local log="$2"
+    local response_body="$3"
+
+    echo "[compare] ${label} request failed"
+    if [[ -s "${response_body}" ]]; then
+        echo
+        echo "[compare] response body:"
+        cat "${response_body}" || true
+    fi
+
+    echo
+    echo "[compare] likely root-cause snippets from ${log}:"
+    grep -n -E \
+        'Traceback|ERROR|CRITICAL|RuntimeError|ValueError|AssertionError|ImportError|ModuleNotFoundError|CUDA|OutOfMemory|No such file|kvcached|TriAttention|TRIATTN_FATAL|no_compactable_groups|compaction_failed' \
+        "${log}" | tail -120 || true
+
+    echo
+    echo "[compare] last 200 log lines from ${log}:"
+    tail -200 "${log}" || true
+}
+
+run_one() {
+    # Note: split into multiple `local` statements — single-line multi-assign
+    # ``local a=$1 b=${a}.x`` trips ``set -u`` on some bash versions.
+    local label="$1"
+    local extra_env="$2"
+    local log="${LOG_DIR}/${label}.log"
+    local result_json="${LOG_DIR}/${label}.json"
+    local response_body="${LOG_DIR}/${label}.response.txt"
+
+    echo
+    echo "===== ${label} ====="
+
+    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
+    env ${extra_env} \
+        vllm serve "${MODEL}" \
+            --served-model-name "${SERVED_MODEL_NAME}" \
+            --dtype bfloat16 --max-model-len "${MAX_LEN}" \
+            --enforce-eager --trust-remote-code \
+            --no-enable-prefix-caching \
+            --gpu-memory-utilization "${GPU_MEM}" \
+            --port "${PORT}" \
+            >"${log}" 2>&1 &
+    local pid=$!
+    trap 'kill '"${pid}"' 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 2; kill -9 '"${pid}"' 2>/dev/null || true' RETURN
+
+    echo "[compare] waiting for ${label} on :${PORT} (up to 8 min)…"
+    if ! wait_ready 480 "${pid}"; then
+        echo "[compare] ${label} failed to start"
+        print_start_failure "${log}"
+        kill "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+        return 1
+    fi
+
+    local mem_before mem_after t0 t1 secs
+    mem_before="$(gpu_used_mib)"
+    t0="$(date +%s.%N)"
+    local http_code
+    http_code="$(
+        curl -sS -o "${response_body}" -w '%{http_code}' \
+            "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/v1/completions" \
+            -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
+            --data-binary @"${BODY_FILE}"
+    )" || {
+        print_runtime_failure "${label}" "${log}" "${response_body}"
+        kill "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+        return 1
+    }
+    if [[ "${http_code}" != "200" ]]; then
+        print_runtime_failure "${label} (HTTP ${http_code})" "${log}" "${response_body}"
+        kill "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+        return 1
+    fi
+    mv "${response_body}" "${result_json}"
+    t1="$(date +%s.%N)"
+    mem_after="$(gpu_used_mib)"
+
+    secs="$("${PYTHON_CMD}" -c "print(${t1} - ${t0})")"
+    local n_compl_tokens
+    n_compl_tokens="$("${PYTHON_CMD}" -c 'import json,sys; d=json.load(open(sys.argv[1])); print(d["usage"]["completion_tokens"])' "${result_json}")"
+    local tps
+    tps="$("${PYTHON_CMD}" -c "print(${n_compl_tokens}/${secs})")"
+    local n_events
+    n_events="$(grep -E -c 'TriAttention compression applied|TriAttention compression summary' "${log}" || true)"
+    local n_freed
+    n_freed="$(grep -c 'TriAttention scheduler FREE_BLOCKS' "${log}" || true)"
+    local n_signals
+    n_signals="$(grep -c 'TriAttention signal triggered' "${log}" || true)"
+
+    printf '  completion tokens  : %s\n' "${n_compl_tokens}"
+    printf '  elapsed seconds    : %.2f\n' "${secs}"
+    printf '  tokens/sec         : %.2f\n' "${tps}"
+    printf '  GPU mem before/after (MiB): %s / %s\n' "${mem_before}" "${mem_after}"
+    printf '  signal log lines   : %s\n' "${n_signals}"
+    printf '  compression-event log lines: %s\n' "${n_events}"
+    printf '  FREE_BLOCKS log lines      : %s\n' "${n_freed}"
+
+    kill "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+    sleep 3
+    kill -9 "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+    trap - RETURN
+
+    # Stash results for the final summary.
+    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' \
+        "${label}" "${n_compl_tokens}" "${secs}" "${tps}" \
+        "${mem_after}" "${n_signals}" "${n_events}" "${n_freed}" \
+        >> "${LOG_DIR}/summary.tsv"
+}
+
+# Run A: kvcached only (baseline).
+run_one "baseline" "ENABLE_KVCACHED=1 \
+    KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH=1 \
+    ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=0 \
+    TRIATTN_RUNTIME_LOG_DECISIONS=0 \
+    TRIATTN_RUNTIME_DISABLE_COMPRESSION=1 \
+    TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH= \
+    TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET="
+
+# Run B: kvcached + TriAttention.
+run_one "triattention" "ENABLE_KVCACHED=1 \
+    KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH=1 \
+    ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=1 \
+    TRIATTN_RUNTIME_LOG_DECISIONS=1 \
+    TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET=${KV_BUDGET} \
+    TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH=${STATS_PATH}"
+
+echo
+echo "================ SUMMARY ================"
+printf '%-15s %10s %10s %10s %10s %10s %10s %10s\n' \
+    "label" "compl_tok" "secs" "tok/s" "gpu_MiB" "signals" "compr_evt" "freed_log"
+while IFS=$'\t' read -r label tok secs tps mem signals evt freed; do
+    printf '%-15s %10s %10.2f %10.2f %10s %10s %10s %10s\n' \
+        "${label}" "${tok}" "${secs}" "${tps}" "${mem}" "${signals}" "${evt}" "${freed}"
+done < "${LOG_DIR}/summary.tsv"
+echo "logs preserved at ${LOG_DIR}"

# ===== END 0095.patch =====

diff --git a/tools/triattention_plot.py b/tools/triattention_plot.py
new file mode 100644

--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/triattention_plot.py
@@ -0,0 +1,98 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: Copyright contributors to the kvcached project
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+"""Plot kvcached + TriAttention sweep results.
+
+Reads the CSV produced by ``tools/triattention_sweep.sh`` and emits three
+side-by-side plots:
+
+  (a) tokens/sec vs max_tokens — main throughput comparison
+  (b) GPU memory after the run vs max_tokens — KV growth story
+  (c) speedup ratio (triattention / baseline) vs max_tokens — trend
+
+Usage:
+    python tools/triattention_plot.py /path/to/results.csv [output.png]
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
+import pandas as pd
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
+    if len(argv) < 2:
+        print(__doc__, file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    csv_path = Path(argv[1])
+    out_path = Path(argv[2]) if len(argv) >= 3 else csv_path.with_suffix(".png")
+
+    df = pd.read_csv(csv_path)
+    df = df.sort_values(["label", "max_tokens"])
+
+    labels = ["baseline", "triattention"]
+    colors = {"baseline": "#1f77b4", "triattention": "#d62728"}
+
+    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(15, 4.2))
+
+    # ---- (a) throughput ----
+    ax = axes[0]
+    for lab in labels:
+        sub = df[df.label == lab]
+        if sub.empty:
+            continue
+        ax.plot(sub.max_tokens, sub.tokens_per_sec,
+                marker="o", color=colors[lab], label=lab, linewidth=2)
+    ax.set_xscale("log", base=2)
+    ax.set_xlabel("max_tokens  (generation length)")
+    ax.set_ylabel("tokens / sec")
+    ax.set_title("Decode throughput")
+    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
+    ax.legend()
+
+    # ---- (b) GPU memory ----
+    ax = axes[1]
+    for lab in labels:
+        sub = df[df.label == lab]
+        if sub.empty:
+            continue
+        ax.plot(sub.max_tokens, sub.gpu_mib_after,
+                marker="s", color=colors[lab], label=lab, linewidth=2)
+    ax.set_xscale("log", base=2)
+    ax.set_xlabel("max_tokens")
+    ax.set_ylabel("GPU memory used after run (MiB)")
+    ax.set_title("GPU memory: KV cache growth")
+    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
+    ax.legend()
+
+    # ---- (c) speedup ratio ----
+    ax = axes[2]
+    base = df[df.label == "baseline"].set_index("max_tokens")["tokens_per_sec"]
+    tri = df[df.label == "triattention"].set_index("max_tokens")["tokens_per_sec"]
+    common = sorted(set(base.index) & set(tri.index))
+    if common:
+        ratios = [tri.loc[k] / base.loc[k] for k in common]
+        ax.plot(common, ratios, marker="^", color="#2ca02c", linewidth=2)
+        ax.axhline(1.0, color="gray", linestyle="--", linewidth=1)
+        ax.set_xscale("log", base=2)
+        ax.set_xlabel("max_tokens")
+        ax.set_ylabel("triattention / baseline (tokens/sec)")
+        ax.set_title("Speedup ratio (>1.0 = TriAttention wins)")
+        ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
+
+    fig.suptitle(
+        f"kvcached + TriAttention vs baseline  |  "
+        f"prompt_repeat={df.prompt_repeat.iloc[0]}  "
+        f"KV_budget={df.kv_budget.iloc[0]}",
+        fontsize=11,
+    )
+    plt.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0, 1, 0.96))
+    plt.savefig(out_path, dpi=160)
+    print(f"saved: {out_path}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv))

# ===== END 0096.patch =====

diff --git a/tools/triattention_smoke.sh b/tools/triattention_smoke.sh
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d54e3cb

+++ b/tools/triattention_smoke.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,167 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: Copyright contributors to the kvcached project
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+#
+# End-to-end smoke test for the merged kvcached + TriAttention stack.
+# Run this on a GPU server with vLLM, torch, and a HuggingFace cache that can
+# fetch the test model.
+#
+# What it covers:
+#   1. Import smoke (no GPU): ``kvcached.triattention`` and its key submodules
+#      can be imported.
+#   2. APC guard:             with ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=1 and prefix caching ON,
+#                             vLLM must refuse to start with the expected error.
+#   3. Happy path:            with ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=1 and prefix caching OFF,
+#                             vLLM must start, print the activation banner,
+#                             serve a /v1/completions request, then shut down.
+#
+# Configure with environment variables (all optional):
+#   MODEL       HuggingFace model id            (default Qwen/Qwen3-8B)
+#   PORT        vLLM serve port                 (default 8765)
+#   GPU_MEM     --gpu-memory-utilization        (default 0.7)
+#   MAX_LEN     --max-model-len                 (default 32768)
+#   STATS_PATH  TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH path
+#               (default: ships-with-package qwen3_32b_int4_stats.pt)
+#
+# Exit code is 0 on success; non-zero on the first failed assertion.
+
+set -euo pipefail
+
+MODEL="${MODEL:-Qwen/Qwen3-8B}"
+PORT="${PORT:-8765}"
+GPU_MEM="${GPU_MEM:-0.7}"
+MAX_LEN="${MAX_LEN:-32768}"
+
+SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
+REPO_ROOT="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
+STATS_PATH="${STATS_PATH:-${REPO_ROOT}/kvcached/triattention/vllm/stats/qwen3_32b_int4_stats.pt}"
+
+LOG_DIR="$(mktemp -d -t triattn-smoke-XXXXXX)"
+trap 'echo "[smoke] logs preserved at ${LOG_DIR}"' EXIT
+
+say() { printf '\n\033[1;36m[smoke] %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
+fail() { printf '\n\033[1;31m[smoke] FAIL: %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; exit 1; }
+pass() { printf '\033[1;32m  ✓ %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
+
+wait_for_port() {
+    local port="$1" timeout="$2" pid="$3"
+    local elapsed=0
+    while (( elapsed < timeout )); do
+        if ! kill -0 "${pid}" 2>/dev/null; then
+            return 2  # server died
+        fi
+        if curl -fsS "http://127.0.0.1:${port}/v1/models" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+            return 0
+        fi
+        sleep 2
+        elapsed=$((elapsed + 2))
+    done
+    return 1
+}
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+say "Step 1/3 — Import smoke (no GPU)"
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+python - <<'PY'
+import importlib, sys
+mods = [
+    "kvcached.triattention",
+    "kvcached.triattention.vllm.runtime.integration_monkeypatch",
+    "kvcached.triattention.sglang",
+]
+for m in mods:
+    importlib.import_module(m)
+    print(f"  ✓ import {m}")
+PY
+pass "all key TriAttention modules importable"
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+say "Step 2/3 — APC guard (must hard-fail)"
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+APC_LOG="${LOG_DIR}/apc_guard.log"
+
+set +e
+ENABLE_KVCACHED=1 \
+KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH=1 \
+ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=1 \
+TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET=2048 \
+TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH="${STATS_PATH}" \
+timeout 180 vllm serve "${MODEL}" \
+    --dtype bfloat16 \
+    --max-model-len "${MAX_LEN}" \
+    --enforce-eager \
+    --trust-remote-code \
+    --enable-prefix-caching \
+    --gpu-memory-utilization "${GPU_MEM}" \
+    --port "$((PORT + 1))" \
+    >"${APC_LOG}" 2>&1
+apc_rc=$?
+set -e
+
+if [[ ${apc_rc} -eq 0 ]]; then
+    fail "vLLM started despite APC + ENABLE_TRIATTENTION (expected refusal). Log: ${APC_LOG}"
+fi
+if ! grep -q "TriAttention is incompatible" "${APC_LOG}"; then
+    fail "APC guard did not surface the expected error. Log: ${APC_LOG}"
+fi
+pass "APC + TriAttention combo refused with the documented error"
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+say "Step 3/3 — Happy path (APC off, ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=1)"
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+HAPPY_LOG="${LOG_DIR}/happy.log"
+
+ENABLE_KVCACHED=1 \
+KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH=1 \
+ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=1 \
+TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET=2048 \
+TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH="${STATS_PATH}" \
+vllm serve "${MODEL}" \
+    --dtype bfloat16 \
+    --max-model-len "${MAX_LEN}" \
+    --enforce-eager \
+    --trust-remote-code \
+    --no-enable-prefix-caching \
+    --gpu-memory-utilization "${GPU_MEM}" \
+    --port "${PORT}" \
+    >"${HAPPY_LOG}" 2>&1 &
+server_pid=$!
+
+cleanup_server() {
+    if kill -0 "${server_pid}" 2>/dev/null; then
+        kill "${server_pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+        sleep 2
+        kill -9 "${server_pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+    fi
+}
+trap 'cleanup_server; echo "[smoke] logs preserved at ${LOG_DIR}"' EXIT
+
+say "  waiting for vLLM to come up on :${PORT} (up to 8 min)…"
+if ! wait_for_port "${PORT}" 480 "${server_pid}"; then
+    fail "vLLM never became ready. Log: ${HAPPY_LOG}"
+fi
+pass "server ready"
+
+if ! grep -q "Runtime plugin activated on top of kvcached" "${HAPPY_LOG}"; then
+    fail "TriAttention activation banner missing from log: ${HAPPY_LOG}"
+fi
+pass "TriAttention activation banner present"
+
+resp="$(curl -fsS "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/v1/completions" \
+    -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
+    -d "{\"model\":\"${MODEL}\",\"prompt\":\"Solve: 1+1=\",\"max_tokens\":16,\"temperature\":0}")"
+
+if [[ -z "${resp}" ]]; then
+    fail "empty completion response"
+fi
+text="$(python -c 'import sys,json; print(json.loads(sys.stdin.read())["choices"][0]["text"])' <<<"${resp}")"
+if [[ -z "${text}" ]]; then
+    fail "completion text was empty. Raw response: ${resp}"
+fi
+pass "completion returned text: $(printf '%s' "${text}" | head -c 80)"
+
+cleanup_server
+trap - EXIT
+
+printf '\n\033[1;32m[smoke] ALL CHECKS PASSED\033[0m\n'
+echo "[smoke] logs preserved at ${LOG_DIR}"

# ===== END 0097.patch =====

diff --git a/tools/triattention_sweep.sh b/tools/triattention_sweep.sh
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9656ffd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/triattention_sweep.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,218 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: Copyright contributors to the kvcached project
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+#
+# Parameter sweep for TriAttention vs baseline (kvcached only). Produces a
+# CSV file you can plot directly.
+#
+# Default sweep: MAX_TOKENS from 128 → 4096 (log-spaced), at fixed
+# PROMPT_REPEAT and KV_BUDGET. For each value we boot vLLM twice (once as
+# baseline, once with TriAttention enabled), send the same long-context
+# request, and record tokens/sec + GPU memory + compression events.
+#
+# Output CSV columns:
+#   label,prompt_repeat,kv_budget,max_tokens,completion_tokens,elapsed_secs,
+#   tokens_per_sec,gpu_mib_after,compression_events,free_blocks_events
+#
+# Usage:
+#   bash tools/triattention_sweep.sh
+#   MODEL=/root/data/models/Qwen/Qwen3-8B bash tools/triattention_sweep.sh
+#
+# Tweak via env vars:
+#   MODEL, PORT, GPU_MEM, MAX_LEN              — same as compare.sh
+#   PROMPT_REPEAT      (default 300)
+#   KV_BUDGET          (default 1024)
+#   MAX_TOKENS_LIST    space-separated list (default "128 256 512 1024 2048 4096")
+#   OUTPUT_CSV         path (default: $LOG_DIR/results.csv)
+#   LABELS             space-separated subset of {baseline,triattention}
+#                      (default both)
+
+set -euo pipefail
+
+MODEL="${MODEL:-Qwen/Qwen3-8B}"
+PORT="${PORT:-8765}"
+GPU_MEM="${GPU_MEM:-0.7}"
+MAX_LEN="${MAX_LEN:-32768}"
+PROMPT_REPEAT="${PROMPT_REPEAT:-300}"
+KV_BUDGET="${KV_BUDGET:-1024}"
+MAX_TOKENS_LIST="${MAX_TOKENS_LIST:-128 256 512 1024 2048 4096}"
+LABELS="${LABELS:-baseline triattention}"
+
+SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
+REPO_ROOT="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
+STATS_PATH="${STATS_PATH:-${REPO_ROOT}/kvcached/triattention/vllm/stats/qwen3_32b_int4_stats.pt}"
+
+LOG_DIR="$(mktemp -d -t triattn-sweep-XXXXXX)"
+OUTPUT_CSV="${OUTPUT_CSV:-${LOG_DIR}/results.csv}"
+echo "[sweep] logs at ${LOG_DIR}"
+echo "[sweep] results CSV: ${OUTPUT_CSV}"
+
+if [[ -n "${PYTHON_BIN:-}" ]]; then
+    PYTHON_CMD="${PYTHON_BIN}"
+elif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+    PYTHON_CMD="python3"
+else
+    PYTHON_CMD="python"
+fi
+
+# Write CSV header.
+echo "label,prompt_repeat,kv_budget,max_tokens,completion_tokens,elapsed_secs,tokens_per_sec,gpu_mib_after,compression_events,free_blocks_events" \
+    > "${OUTPUT_CSV}"
+
+wait_ready() {
+    local timeout=$1 pid=$2 elapsed=0
+    while (( elapsed < timeout )); do
+        if ! kill -0 "${pid}" 2>/dev/null; then return 2; fi
+        if curl -fsS "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/v1/models" >/dev/null 2>&1; then return 0; fi
+        sleep 2; elapsed=$((elapsed + 2))
+    done
+    return 1
+}
+
+gpu_used_mib() {
+    nvidia-smi --query-gpu=memory.used --format=csv,noheader,nounits | head -1 | tr -d ' '
+}
+
+build_body() {
+    local max_tokens="$1" body_file="$2"
+    MODEL="${MODEL}" MAX_TOKENS="${max_tokens}" PROMPT_REPEAT="${PROMPT_REPEAT}" \
+        "${PYTHON_CMD}" - "${body_file}" <<'PY'
+import json, os, sys
+seed = "The capital of France is Paris. "
+repeat = int(os.environ["PROMPT_REPEAT"])
+prompt = seed * repeat + "Now in one short sentence, what is the capital of France?"
+body = {
+    "model": os.environ["MODEL"],
+    "prompt": prompt,
+    "max_tokens": int(os.environ["MAX_TOKENS"]),
+    "temperature": 0,
+}
+with open(sys.argv[1], "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+    json.dump(body, f)
+PY
+}
+
+env_for_label() {
+    case "$1" in
+        baseline)
+            echo "ENABLE_KVCACHED=1 KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH=1"
+            ;;
+        triattention)
+            echo "ENABLE_KVCACHED=1 KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH=1 ENABLE_TRIATTENTION=1 TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET=${KV_BUDGET} TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH=${STATS_PATH}"
+            ;;
+        *)
+            echo "" ;;
+    esac
+}
+
+run_cell() {
+    local label="$1" max_tokens="$2"
+    local cell_id="${label}-max${max_tokens}"
+    local log="${LOG_DIR}/${cell_id}.log"
+    local body_file="${LOG_DIR}/${cell_id}.body.json"
+    local resp_file="${LOG_DIR}/${cell_id}.resp.json"
+
+    echo
+    echo "===== ${cell_id} ====="
+    build_body "${max_tokens}" "${body_file}"
+
+    local extra_env
+    extra_env="$(env_for_label "${label}")"
+
+    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
+    env ${extra_env} \
+        vllm serve "${MODEL}" \
+            --dtype bfloat16 --max-model-len "${MAX_LEN}" \
+            --enforce-eager --trust-remote-code \
+            --no-enable-prefix-caching \
+            --gpu-memory-utilization "${GPU_MEM}" \
+            --port "${PORT}" \
+            >"${log}" 2>&1 &
+    local pid=$!
+    trap 'kill '"${pid}"' 2>/dev/null || true; sleep 2; kill -9 '"${pid}"' 2>/dev/null || true' RETURN
+
+    echo "[sweep]   waiting for vllm (up to 8 min)…"
+    if ! wait_ready 480 "${pid}"; then
+        echo "[sweep]   ${cell_id} failed to start; tail log:"
+        tail -40 "${log}" || true
+        kill "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+        sleep 2
+        trap - RETURN
+        return 1
+    fi
+
+    local mem_before t0 t1 secs http_code
+    mem_before="$(gpu_used_mib)"
+    t0="$(date +%s.%N)"
+    http_code="$(
+        curl -sS -o "${resp_file}" -w '%{http_code}' \
+            "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/v1/completions" \
+            -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
+            --data-binary @"${body_file}"
+    )" || http_code="000"
+    t1="$(date +%s.%N)"
+    local mem_after
+    mem_after="$(gpu_used_mib)"
+
+    if [[ "${http_code}" != "200" ]]; then
+        echo "[sweep]   HTTP ${http_code}; response:"
+        cat "${resp_file}" 2>/dev/null || true
+        echo "[sweep]   tail of server log:"
+        tail -40 "${log}" || true
+        kill "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+        sleep 2
+        trap - RETURN
+        return 1
+    fi
+
+    secs="$("${PYTHON_CMD}" -c "print(${t1} - ${t0})")"
+    local n_compl
+    n_compl="$("${PYTHON_CMD}" -c \
+        'import json,sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["usage"]["completion_tokens"])' \
+        "${resp_file}")"
+    local tps
+    tps="$("${PYTHON_CMD}" -c "print(${n_compl}/${secs} if ${secs} > 0 else 0)")"
+    local n_events n_freed
+    n_events="$(grep -c 'TriAttention compression applied' "${log}" || true)"
+    n_freed="$(grep -c 'TriAttention scheduler FREE_BLOCKS' "${log}" || true)"
+
+    printf '  completion_tokens : %s\n' "${n_compl}"
+    printf '  elapsed_secs      : %.2f\n' "${secs}"
+    printf '  tokens_per_sec    : %.2f\n' "${tps}"
+    printf '  gpu_mib_after     : %s\n' "${mem_after}"
+    printf '  compression_events: %s\n' "${n_events}"
+    printf '  free_blocks       : %s\n' "${n_freed}"
+
+    printf '%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%.3f,%.3f,%s,%s,%s\n' \
+        "${label}" "${PROMPT_REPEAT}" "${KV_BUDGET}" "${max_tokens}" \
+        "${n_compl}" "${secs}" "${tps}" "${mem_after}" \
+        "${n_events}" "${n_freed}" \
+        >> "${OUTPUT_CSV}"
+
+    kill "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+    sleep 3
+    kill -9 "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+    trap - RETURN
+}
+
+declare -i total=0 ok=0
+for max_tokens in ${MAX_TOKENS_LIST}; do
+    for label in ${LABELS}; do
+        total=$((total + 1))
+        if run_cell "${label}" "${max_tokens}"; then
+            ok=$((ok + 1))
+        else
+            echo "[sweep]   cell ${label}-max${max_tokens} FAILED (see logs in ${LOG_DIR})"
+        fi
+    done
+done
+
+echo
+echo "================ SWEEP DONE ================"
+echo "cells ok  : ${ok}/${total}"
+echo "results   : ${OUTPUT_CSV}"
+echo "logs      : ${LOG_DIR}"
+
+echo
+echo "preview:"
+column -t -s, "${OUTPUT_CSV}" | head -40

# ===== END 0098.patch =====

diff --git a/tools/triattention_aime25_eval.py b/tools/triattention_aime25_eval.py

index 0000000..a28adef

+++ b/tools/triattention_aime25_eval.py

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: Copyright contributors to the kvcached project
+# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+"""Evaluate kvcached vs kvcached + TriAttention on a small AIME25 subset.
+
+The script starts one fresh vLLM server per label, sends the same AIME
+questions to each server, samples GPU memory with nvidia-smi, and writes:
+
+  - summary.csv: one row per label
+  - responses.jsonl: one row per request
+  - <label>.log: raw vLLM server log
+  - <label>_mem.csv: timestamped GPU memory samples
+
+Example:
+  python tools/triattention_aime25_eval.py \
+    --model /root/data/models/Qwen/Qwen3-8B \
+    --served-model-name Qwen3-8B \
+    --dataset /root/data/datasets/aime25/aime25_subset_10.jsonl \
+    --stats-path /root/kvcached_tri/kvcached/triattention/calibration/for_aime25_experiment/qwen3_8b.pt
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import csv
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import signal
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import threading
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+
+REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+DEFAULT_DATASET = Path("/root/data/datasets/aime25/aime25_subset_10.jsonl")
+DEFAULT_STATS = (
+    REPO_ROOT
+    / "kvcached"
+    / "triattention"
+    / "calibration"
+    / "for_aime25_experiment"
+    / "qwen3_8b.pt"
+)
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Example:
+    index: int
+    question: str
+    answer: str
+    metadata: dict[str, Any]
+
+
+@dataclass
+class RequestResult:
+    label: str
+    index: int
+    expected: str
+    predicted: str | None
+    correct: bool
+    elapsed_secs: float
+    prompt_tokens: int | None
+    completion_tokens: int | None
+    total_tokens: int | None
+    output_text: str
+    error: str | None = None
+
+
+class GpuMemorySampler:
+    def __init__(self, output_csv: Path, interval: float, gpu_index: str | None) -> None:
+        self.output_csv = output_csv
+        self.interval = interval
+        self.gpu_index = gpu_index
+        self._stop = threading.Event()
+        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
+        self.samples: list[tuple[float, int]] = []
+
+    def start(self) -> None:
+        self.output_csv.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
+        self._thread.start()
+
+    def stop(self) -> None:
+        self._stop.set()
+        if self._thread is not None:
+            self._thread.join(timeout=2)
+
+    @property
+    def peak_mib(self) -> int | None:
+        if not self.samples:
+            return None
+        return max(value for _, value in self.samples)
+
+    @property
+    def latest_mib(self) -> int | None:
+        if not self.samples:
+            return None
+        return self.samples[-1][1]
+
+    def peak_since(self, timestamp: float) -> int | None:
+        values = [value for ts, value in self.samples if ts >= timestamp]
+        if not values:
+            return None
+        return max(values)
+
+    def _run(self) -> None:
+        cmd = [
+            "nvidia-smi",
+            "--query-gpu=memory.used",
+            "--format=csv,noheader,nounits",
+        ]
+        if self.gpu_index:
+            cmd.extend(["-i", self.gpu_index])
+        with self.output_csv.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
+            writer = csv.writer(f)
+            writer.writerow(["timestamp", "memory_used_mib"])
+            while not self._stop.is_set():
+                ts = time.time()
+                try:
+                    out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, text=True, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
+                    first = out.strip().splitlines()[0].strip()
+                    mib = int(first)
+                    self.samples.append((ts, mib))
+                    writer.writerow([f"{ts:.6f}", mib])
+                    f.flush()
+                except Exception:
+                    writer.writerow([f"{ts:.6f}", ""])
+                    f.flush()
+                self._stop.wait(self.interval)
+
+
+def parse_args(argv: list[str]) -> argparse.Namespace:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    parser.add_argument("--model", default=os.environ.get("MODEL", "/root/data/models/Qwen/Qwen3-8B"))
+    parser.add_argument("--served-model-name", default=os.environ.get("SERVED_MODEL_NAME", "Qwen3-8B"))
+    parser.add_argument("--dataset", type=Path, default=Path(os.environ.get("DATASET", DEFAULT_DATASET)))
+    parser.add_argument("--stats-path", type=Path, default=Path(os.environ.get("STATS_PATH", DEFAULT_STATS)))
+    parser.add_argument("--labels", default=os.environ.get("LABELS", "baseline,triattention"))
+    parser.add_argument("--max-examples", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("MAX_EXAMPLES", "10")))
+    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("PORT", "12346")))
+    parser.add_argument("--gpu-memory-utilization", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("GPU_MEM", "0.7")))
+    parser.add_argument("--max-model-len", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("MAX_LEN", "32768")))
+    parser.add_argument("--dtype", default=os.environ.get("DTYPE", "bfloat16"))
+    parser.add_argument("--kv-budget", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("KV_BUDGET", "1024")))
+    parser.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("MAX_TOKENS", "2048")))
+    parser.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("TEMPERATURE", "0")))
+    parser.add_argument("--vllm-bin", default=os.environ.get("VLLM_BIN", "vllm"))
+    parser.add_argument("--server-start-timeout", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("SERVER_START_TIMEOUT", "480")))
+    parser.add_argument("--request-timeout", type=int, default=int(os.environ.get("REQUEST_TIMEOUT", "3600")))
+    parser.add_argument("--gpu-sample-interval", type=float, default=float(os.environ.get("GPU_SAMPLE_INTERVAL", "0.2")))
+    parser.add_argument("--gpu-index", default=os.environ.get("GPU_INDEX"))
+    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=Path(os.environ["OUTPUT_DIR"]) if os.environ.get("OUTPUT_DIR") else None)
+    parser.add_argument("--extra-vllm-arg", action="append", default=[])
+    parser.add_argument("--no-enforce-eager", dest="enforce_eager", action="store_false")
+    parser.set_defaults(enforce_eager=True)
+    return parser.parse_args(argv)
+
+
+def load_examples(path: Path, max_examples: int) -> list[Example]:
+    examples: list[Example] = []
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        for i, line in enumerate(f):
+            if max_examples > 0 and len(examples) >= max_examples:
+                break
+            if not line.strip():
+                continue
+            row = json.loads(line)
+            examples.append(
+                Example(
+                    index=i,
+                    question=str(row["question"]),
+                    answer=str(row["answer"]),
+                    metadata=dict(row.get("metadata") or {}),
+                )
+            )
+    return examples
+
+
+def build_prompt(question: str) -> str:
+    return (
+        "Solve the following AIME problem. Reason step by step. "
+        "At the end, write exactly one line in this format:\n"
+        "Final answer: <integer from 0 to 999>\n\n"
+        f"Problem:\n{question}\n"
+    )
+
+
+def normalize_answer(value: str | None) -> str | None:
+    if value is None:
+        return None
+    match = re.search(r"\d{1,3}", value)
+    if not match:
+        return None
+    return str(int(match.group(0)))
+
+
+def extract_answer(text: str) -> str | None:
+    patterns = [
+        r"final answer\s*[:：]\s*\$?\\boxed\{?(\d{1,3})\}?\$?",
+        r"final answer\s*[:：]\s*(\d{1,3})",
+        r"\\boxed\{(\d{1,3})\}",
+    ]
+    for pattern in patterns:
+        match = re.search(pattern, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
+        if match:
+            return normalize_answer(match.group(1))
+    matches = re.findall(r"\b\d{1,3}\b", text)
+    if matches:
+        return normalize_answer(matches[-1])
+    return None
+
+
+def wait_ready(port: int, timeout: int, proc: subprocess.Popen[Any], log_path: Path) -> bool:
+    deadline = time.time() + timeout
+    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/v1/models"
+    next_notice = time.time()
+    while time.time() < deadline:
+        if proc.poll() is not None:
+            return False
+        try:
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=2) as resp:
+                if resp.status == 200:
+                    return True
+        except Exception:
+            now = time.time()
+            if now >= next_notice:
+                remaining = max(0, int(deadline - now))
+                print(
+                    f"[wait] vLLM not ready on :{port}; "
+                    f"{remaining}s left. log={log_path}",
+                    flush=True,
+                )
+                next_notice = now + 20
+            time.sleep(2)
+    return False
+
+
+def stop_process(proc: subprocess.Popen[Any]) -> None:
+    if proc.poll() is not None:
+        return
+    try:
+        proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+    except Exception:
+        try:
+            proc.kill()
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        try:
+            proc.wait(timeout=5)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+
+
+def build_env(label: str, args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, str]:
+    env = os.environ.copy()
+    for key in list(env):
+        if key == "ENABLE_TRIATTENTION" or key.startswith("TRIATTN_RUNTIME_"):
+            env.pop(key, None)
+    env.update(
+        {
+            "ENABLE_KVCACHED": "1",
+            "KVCACHED_AUTOPATCH": "1",
+        }
+    )
+    if label == "baseline":
+        env.update(
+            {
+                "ENABLE_TRIATTENTION": "0",
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_DISABLE_COMPRESSION": "1",
+            }
+        )
+    elif label == "triattention":
+        env.update(
+            {
+                "ENABLE_TRIATTENTION": "1",
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_KV_BUDGET": str(args.kv_budget),
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_SPARSE_STATS_PATH": str(args.stats_path),
+                "TRIATTN_RUNTIME_LOG_DECISIONS": "1",
+            }
+        )
+    else:
+        raise ValueError(f"unknown label: {label}")
+    return env
+
+
+def build_vllm_cmd(args: argparse.Namespace) -> list[str]:
+    cmd = [
+        args.vllm_bin,
+        "serve",
+        args.model,
+        "--served-model-name",
+        args.served_model_name,
+        "--dtype",
+        args.dtype,
+        "--max-model-len",
+        str(args.max_model_len),
+        "--trust-remote-code",
+        "--no-enable-prefix-caching",
+        "--gpu-memory-utilization",
+        str(args.gpu_memory_utilization),
+        "--port",
+        str(args.port),
+    ]
+    if args.enforce_eager:
+        cmd.append("--enforce-eager")
+    cmd.extend(args.extra_vllm_arg or [])
+    return cmd
+
+
+def post_completion(
+    args: argparse.Namespace,
+    prompt: str,
+) -> tuple[str, int | None, int | None, int | None]:
+    body = {
+        "model": args.served_model_name,
+        "prompt": prompt,
+        "max_tokens": args.max_tokens,
+        "temperature": args.temperature,
+    }
+    data = json.dumps(body).encode("utf-8")
+    req = urllib.request.Request(
+        f"http://127.0.0.1:{args.port}/v1/completions",
+        data=data,
+        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
+        method="POST",
+    )
+    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=args.request_timeout) as resp:
+        payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
+    text = payload["choices"][0]["text"]
+    prompt_tokens = None
+    completion_tokens = None
+    total_tokens = None
+    usage = payload.get("usage")
+    if isinstance(usage, dict):
+        if "prompt_tokens" in usage:
+            prompt_tokens = int(usage["prompt_tokens"])
+        if "completion_tokens" in usage:
+            completion_tokens = int(usage["completion_tokens"])
+        if "total_tokens" in usage:
+            total_tokens = int(usage["total_tokens"])
+    return text, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, total_tokens
+
+
+def count_log_events(log_path: Path) -> dict[str, int]:
+    if not log_path.exists():
+        return {"signals": 0, "compressions": 0, "free_blocks": 0}
+    text = log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
+    return {
+        "signals": text.count("TriAttention signal triggered"),
+        "compressions": len(
+            re.findall(
+                r"TriAttention compression applied|TriAttention compression summary",
+                text,
+            )
+        ),
+        "free_blocks": text.count("TriAttention scheduler FREE_BLOCKS"),
+    }
+
+
+def run_label(
+    *,
+    label: str,
+    args: argparse.Namespace,
+    examples: list[Example],
+    output_dir: Path,
+) -> tuple[list[RequestResult], dict[str, Any]]:
+    log_path = output_dir / f"{label}.log"
+    mem_path = output_dir / f"{label}_mem.csv"
+    env = build_env(label, args)
+    cmd = build_vllm_cmd(args)
+
+    print(f"\n===== {label} =====", flush=True)
+    print("env:", " ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in build_env(label, args).items() if k.startswith(("ENABLE_", "KVCACHED_", "TRIATTN_"))), flush=True)
+    print("cmd:", " ".join(cmd), flush=True)
+    log_file = log_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8")
+    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, env=env, stdout=log_file, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
+    sampler = GpuMemorySampler(mem_path, args.gpu_sample_interval, args.gpu_index)
+    sampler.start()
+
+    try:
+        if not wait_ready(args.port, args.server_start_timeout, proc, log_path):
+            tail = ""
+            if log_path.exists():
+                tail = "\n".join(log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines()[-120:])
+            raise RuntimeError(f"{label} server failed to become ready. Log tail:\n{tail}")
+
+        ready_gpu_mib = sampler.latest_mib
+        request_start_ts = time.time()
+        results: list[RequestResult] = []
+        for example in examples:
+            prompt = build_prompt(example.question)
+            print(f"[{label}] problem {example.index + 1}: expected={example.answer}", flush=True)
+            start = time.time()
+            try:
+                output_text, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, total_tokens = post_completion(args, prompt)
+                elapsed = time.time() - start
+                predicted = extract_answer(output_text)
+                correct = normalize_answer(example.answer) == predicted
+                result = RequestResult(
+                    label=label,
+                    index=example.index,
+                    expected=normalize_answer(example.answer) or example.answer,
+                    predicted=predicted,
+                    correct=bool(correct),
+                    elapsed_secs=elapsed,
+                    prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens,
+                    completion_tokens=completion_tokens,
+                    total_tokens=total_tokens,
+                    output_text=output_text,
+                )
+                status = "OK" if correct else "MISS"
+                print(
+                    f"[{label}]   {status} predicted={predicted} "
+                    f"elapsed={elapsed:.2f}s "
+                    f"prompt_tokens={prompt_tokens} completion_tokens={completion_tokens}",
+                    flush=True,
+                )
+            except (urllib.error.HTTPError, urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, Exception) as exc:
+                elapsed = time.time() - start
+                result = RequestResult(
+                    label=label,
+                    index=example.index,
+                    expected=normalize_answer(example.answer) or example.answer,
+                    predicted=None,
+                    correct=False,
+                    elapsed_secs=elapsed,
+                    prompt_tokens=None,
+                    completion_tokens=None,
+                    total_tokens=None,
+                    output_text="",
+                    error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
+                )
+                print(f"[{label}]   ERROR {result.error}", flush=True)
+            results.append(result)
+
+        events = count_log_events(log_path)
+        total_elapsed = sum(r.elapsed_secs for r in results)
+        total_prompt_tokens = sum(r.prompt_tokens or 0 for r in results)
+        total_completion_tokens = sum(r.completion_tokens or 0 for r in results)
+        total_tokens = sum(
+            r.total_tokens if r.total_tokens is not None else (r.prompt_tokens or 0) + (r.completion_tokens or 0)
+            for r in results
+        )
+        summary = {
+            "label": label,
+            "num_examples": len(results),
+            "num_correct": sum(1 for r in results if r.correct),
+            "accuracy": sum(1 for r in results if r.correct) / len(results) if results else 0.0,
+            "total_elapsed_secs": total_elapsed,
+            "avg_elapsed_secs": total_elapsed / len(results) if results else 0.0,
+            "total_prompt_tokens": total_prompt_tokens,
+            "total_completion_tokens": total_completion_tokens,
+            "total_tokens": total_tokens,
+            "completion_tokens_per_sec": total_completion_tokens / total_elapsed if total_elapsed > 0 else 0.0,
+            "total_tokens_per_sec": total_tokens / total_elapsed if total_elapsed > 0 else 0.0,
+            "ready_gpu_mib": ready_gpu_mib,
+            "request_peak_gpu_mib": sampler.peak_since(request_start_ts),
+            "peak_gpu_mib": sampler.peak_mib,
+            **events,
+            "log_path": str(log_path),
+            "mem_path": str(mem_path),
+        }
+        return results, summary
+    finally:
+        stop_process(proc)
+        sampler.stop()
+        log_file.close()
+        time.sleep(3)
+
+
+def write_outputs(
+    output_dir: Path,
+    all_results: list[RequestResult],
+    summaries: list[dict[str, Any]],
+) -> None:
+    responses_path = output_dir / "responses.jsonl"
+    with responses_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        for result in all_results:
+            f.write(json.dumps(result.__dict__, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
+
+    summary_path = output_dir / "summary.csv"
+    fieldnames = [
+        "label",
+        "num_examples",
+        "num_correct",
+        "accuracy",
+        "total_elapsed_secs",
+        "avg_elapsed_secs",
+        "total_prompt_tokens",
+        "total_completion_tokens",
+        "total_tokens",
+        "completion_tokens_per_sec",
+        "total_tokens_per_sec",
+        "ready_gpu_mib",
+        "request_peak_gpu_mib",
+        "peak_gpu_mib",
+        "signals",
+        "compressions",
+        "free_blocks",
+        "log_path",
+        "mem_path",
+    ]
+    with summary_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
+        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=fieldnames)
+        writer.writeheader()
+        for row in summaries:
+            writer.writerow({key: row.get(key) for key in fieldnames})
+
+    print("\n================ SUMMARY ================")
+    print(
+        f"{'label':<14} {'avg_s':>9} {'comp_t/s':>10} {'total_t/s':>10} "
+        f"{'ready_mib':>10} {'req_peak':>10} {'peak_mib':>10} "
+        f"{'signals':>8} {'compr':>8} {'freed':>8}"
+    )
+    for row in summaries:
+        print(
+            f"{row['label']:<14} {row['avg_elapsed_secs']:>9.2f} "
+            f"{row['completion_tokens_per_sec']:>10.2f} "
+            f"{row['total_tokens_per_sec']:>10.2f} "
+            f"{str(row['ready_gpu_mib']):>10} "
+            f"{str(row['request_peak_gpu_mib']):>10} "
+            f"{str(row['peak_gpu_mib']):>10} "
+            f"{row['signals']:>8} {row['compressions']:>8} {row['free_blocks']:>8}"
+        )
+    print(f"\nsummary:   {summary_path}")
+    print(f"responses: {responses_path}")
+    print(f"logs:      {output_dir}")
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
+    args = parse_args(argv)
+    if not args.dataset.exists():
+        raise SystemExit(f"dataset not found: {args.dataset}")
+    labels = [item.strip() for item in args.labels.split(",") if item.strip()]
+    unknown = set(labels) - {"baseline", "triattention"}
+    if unknown:
+        raise SystemExit(f"unknown labels: {', '.join(sorted(unknown))}")
+    if "triattention" in labels and not args.stats_path.exists():
+        raise SystemExit(f"stats file not found: {args.stats_path}")
+
+    output_dir = args.output_dir or Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="triattn-aime25-"))
+    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    examples = load_examples(args.dataset, args.max_examples)
+    if not examples:
+        raise SystemExit(f"no examples loaded from {args.dataset}")
+
+    print(f"[aime25] output dir: {output_dir}", flush=True)
+    print(f"[aime25] dataset: {args.dataset}", flush=True)
+    print(f"[aime25] examples: {len(examples)}", flush=True)
+    print(f"[aime25] model: {args.model}", flush=True)
+    print(f"[aime25] served model name: {args.served_model_name}", flush=True)
+    print(f"[aime25] stats path: {args.stats_path}", flush=True)
+    print(f"[aime25] labels: {labels}", flush=True)
+
+    all_results: list[RequestResult] = []
+    summaries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+    for label in labels:
+        results, summary = run_label(
+            label=label,
+            args=args,
+            examples=examples,
+            output_dir=output_dir,
+        )
+        all_results.extend(results)
+        summaries.append(summary)
+
+    write_outputs(output_dir, all_results, summaries)
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

# ===== END 0110.patch =====
